	{

			case 24:

	public void EncourageDespawn(int despawnTime)
	{
		if (timeLeft > despawnTime)
		{
			timeLeft = despawnTime;
		}
		despawnEncouraged = true;
	}

	public void DiscourageDespawn(int despawnTime)
	{
		if (timeLeft < despawnTime)
		{
			timeLeft = despawnTime;
		}
		despawnEncouraged = false;
	}

	private void CreateBrickBoxForWallOfFlesh()
	{
		int num = (int)(position.X + (float)(width / 2)) / 16;
		int num2 = (int)(position.Y + (float)(height / 2)) / 16;
		int num3 = width / 2 / 16 + 1;
		for (int i = num - num3; i <= num + num3; i++)
		{
			for (int j = num2 - num3; j <= num2 + num3; j++)
			{
				if ((i == num - num3 || i == num + num3 || j == num2 - num3 || j == num2 + num3) && !Main.tile[i, j].active())
				{
					Main.tile[i, j].type = (ushort)(WorldGen.crimson ? 347 : 140);
					Main.tile[i, j].active(active: true);
				}
				Main.tile[i, j].lava(lava: false);
				Main.tile[i, j].liquid = 0;
				if (Main.netMode == 2)
				{
					NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, i, j);
				}
				else
				{
					WorldGen.SquareTileFrame(i, j);
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void RegisterBoss_Plantera()
	{
		short type = 262;
		RegisterToNPC(type, ItemDropRule.BossBag(3328));
		RegisterToNPC(type, ItemDropRule.MasterModeCommonDrop(4934));
		RegisterToNPC(type, ItemDropRule.MasterModeDropOnAllPlayers(4806, _masterModeDropRng));
		LeadingConditionRule leadingConditionRule = new LeadingConditionRule(new Conditions.NotExpert());
		RegisterToNPC(type, leadingConditionRule);
		LeadingConditionRule leadingConditionRule2 = new LeadingConditionRule(new Conditions.FirstTimeKillingPlantera());
		leadingConditionRule.OnSuccess(leadingConditionRule2);
		leadingConditionRule.OnSuccess(ItemDropRule.Common(2109, 7));
		leadingConditionRule.OnSuccess(ItemDropRule.Common(1141));
		leadingConditionRule.OnSuccess(ItemDropRule.Common(1182, 20));

	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(float normalDamage)
	{
		return (int)(normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(difficulty));
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_CappedAtMaster(float normalDamage)
	{
		float num = Math.Min(difficulty, GameDifficultyLevel.Master);
		return (int)(normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(num));
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_LerpBetweenFinalValues(float normalDamage, float expertDamage)
	{
		return (int)Utils.Remap(difficulty, GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, normalDamage, expertDamage);
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ForProjectiles(float normalDamage, float expertDamage)
	{
		return (int)Utils.Remap(difficulty, GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, normalDamage, expertDamage);
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ForProjectiles_MultiLerp(float normalDamage, float expertDamage, float masterDamage)
	{
		return (int)Utils.MultiLerp(Utils.GetLerpValue(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, GameDifficultyLevel.Master, difficulty, clamped: true), normalDamage, expertDamage, masterDamage);
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ForTownNPC(float normalDamage)
	{
		return (int)(normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.TownNPCDamageMultiplier.Sample(Main.Difficulty));
	}

```

	public static int GetAvailableAmountOfNPCsToSpawnUpToSlot(int amountWeWant, int highestNPCSlotIndexWeWillPick = 100)
	{
		if (amountWeWant <= 0)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < highestNPCSlotIndexWeWillPick; i++)
		{
			if (!Main.npc[i].active)
			{
				num++;
				if (num >= amountWeWant)
				{
					return amountWeWant;
				}
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	public static void SpawnBoss(int spawnPositionX, int spawnPositionY, int Type, int targetPlayerIndex, float ai0 = 0f, float ai1 = 0f, float ai2 = 0f, float ai3 = 0f)
	{
		int maxNPCs = Main.maxNPCs;
		bool flag = Type == 134 || Type == 127 || Type == 126 || Type == 125;
		if (flag)
		{
			AchievementsHelper.MechaMayhem_Clear();
		}
		if (Type == 127 && mechQueen != -1)
		{
			maxNPCs = (mechQueen = NewNPC(GetBossSpawnSource(targetPlayerIndex), spawnPositionX, spawnPositionY, Type, 100, ai0, ai1, ai2, ai3, targetPlayerIndex));
			Main.npc[mechQueen].ai[3] = mechQueen;
		}
		else
		{
			maxNPCs = NewNPC(GetBossSpawnSource(targetPlayerIndex), spawnPositionX, spawnPositionY, Type, 1, ai0, ai1, ai2, ai3, targetPlayerIndex);

		if (maxNPCs == Main.maxNPCs)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			AchievementsHelper.MechaMayhem_Start();
		}
		Main.npc[maxNPCs].timeLeft *= 20;
		_ = Main.npc[maxNPCs].TypeName;
		if (Main.netMode == 2 && maxNPCs < Main.maxNPCs)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(23, -1, -1, null, maxNPCs);
		}
		if (Type == 127 && mechQueen == maxNPCs)
		{
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(Lang.misc[107].ToNetworkText(), ChatColors.BossOrEvent);
			return;
		}
		switch (Type)
		{
		case 125:
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(Lang.misc[48].ToNetworkText(), ChatColors.BossOrEvent);
			break;
		default:
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(NetworkText.FromKey("Announcement.HasAwoken", Main.npc[maxNPCs].GetTypeNetName()), ChatColors.BossOrEvent);
			break;
		case 50:
		case 82:
		case 126:
		case 316:
		case 398:
		case 551:
		case 662:
			break;
		}
	}

**📎 ToolResult**

```
namespace Terraria.DataStructures;

public static class GameDifficultyData
{
	public struct LinearCurve
	{
		public struct Key(float input, float output)
		{
			public readonly float input = input;

			public readonly float output = output;

			public override string ToString()
			{
				return input + " -> " + output;
			}
		}

		public readonly Key[] keys;

		public LinearCurve(params Key[] keys)
		{
			this.keys = keys;
			_ = ref keys[0];
			for (int i = 1; i < keys.Length; i++)
			{
				_ = keys[i].input;
			}
		}

		public float Sample(float value)
		{
			Key key = keys[0];
			Key key2 = key;
			for (int i = 0; i < keys.Length; i++)
			{
				key2 = keys[i];
				if (value <= key2.input)
				{
					break;
				}
				key = key2;
			}
			float num = key2.input - key.input;
			float num2 = key2.output - key.output;
			if (num == 0f)
			{
				return key.output;
			}
			return (value - key.input) * num2 / num + key.output;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return string.Join(", ", keys);
		}
	}

	public static readonly LinearCurve EnemyMaxLifeMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Journey, 0.5f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Legendary, 4f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve EnemyDamageMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Journey, 0.5f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Master, 3f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Legendary, 5.3333335f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve HostileProjectileDamageMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Journey, 0.5f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Master, 3f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve KnockbackToEnemiesMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, 1f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Master, 0.8f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve EnemyMoneyDropMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, 1f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, 2.5f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Master, 2.5f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Legendary, 3.5f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve TownNPCDamageMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Journey, 2f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, 1f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, 1.5f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Legendary, 2f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve DebuffTimeMultiplier = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, 1f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, 2f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Master, 2.5f));

	public static readonly LinearCurve LightningPlayerDamageScaling = new LinearCurve(new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Journey, 0.04f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, 0.08f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Master, 0.24f), new LinearCurve.Key(GameDifficultyLevel.Legendary, 0.4f));
}

All findings gathered. Here is the complete authoritative spec.

---

# Plantera (世纪之花) Chain — Terraria 1.4.5.6 decompiled authority (`Terarria1456/`)

**Important ID shift note for 1456:** NPC IDs are shifted +? relative to 1.4.4-era tables. In this tree:
- `NPCID.Plantera = 262` (`Terraria.ID/NPCID.cs:11589`), `PlanterasHook = 263` (:11591), `PlanterasTentacle = 264` (:11593), `Spore = 265` (:11595) — names confirmed in the name table at `NPCID.cs:10725-10728`.
- `TileID.PlanteraBulb = 238` (`Terraria.ID/TileID.cs:909`).
- `ProjectileID.SeedPlantera = 275`, `PoisonSeedPlantera = 276`, `ThornBall = 277` (`Terraria.ID/ProjectileID.cs:910/912/914`).
- Treasure bag is `ItemID.PlanteraBossBag = 3328` (`Terraria.ID/ItemID.cs:8195`) — **not** 3331 (3331 = CultistBossBag).
- Music is `MusicID.Plantera = 24` (`Terraria.ID/MusicID.cs:60`); `MusicID.Boss5 = 25` (:62) is a different track.

---

## 1. Plantera's Bulb tile (238)

- Value: `TileID.PlanteraBulb = 238` — `Terraria.ID/TileID.cs:909`.
- **No entries in `TileID.Sets`** for 238 (grep `\[[^\]]*238[^\]]*\]` in TileID.cs returns nothing).
- Properties set in `Main.InitializeTileDefinitions` region (`Terraria/Main.cs`):
  - `tileLavaDeath[238] = true` — `Main.cs:8117`
  - `tileFrameImportant[238] = true` — `Main.cs:10144`
  - `tileLighted[238] = true` — `Main.cs:10154`
  - Not solid, not cut, no anchor sets, no `tileDungeon`.
- Animation: 4-frame cycle, advance every 21 ticks — `Main.cs:18559-18567` (`tileFrameCounter[238]++`, `>20` → `tileFrame[238]++`, wrap `>= 4` → 0).
- Framing/hitbox handled as a 2×2 jungle plant: `WorldGen.CheckJunglePlant` is dispatched from `TileFrame` for types 233/236/238/702 — `Terraria/WorldGen.cs:86219-86223`.
- Placement helper: `WorldGen.PlaceJunglePlant(X, Y, type, styleX, styleY, inheritPaint)` — `WorldGen.cs:47785`. For type 238 it writes a 2×2 block at `(X-1..X, Y-1..Y)`, `frameX = 0 / 18`, `frameY = 0 / 18` (`WorldGen.cs:47842-47870`), requires **SolidTile of type 60 (Jungle Grass)** directly below both columns (`WorldGen.cs:47820-47827`), requires the 2×2 area to be clear except vines/mushroom plants/jungle plants 233 (`WorldGen.cs:47795-47812`), inherits paint from the grass below (47873-47881), kills any tile in the 2×2 first (47834-47842).
- Also placeable from an item via `WorldGen.PlaceTile` → `case 238:` → `PlaceJunglePlant(i, j, 238, 0, 0, inheritPaint: true)` — `WorldGen.cs:60032-60036`.
- Drop item: **none** (no item drop for tile 238). Mine sound: `WorldGen.cs:68820-68823` → `num = genRand.Next(3) != 0 ? 166 : 167` (two grass-style dig sounds).

---

## 2. Bulb spawning in-game

### A. Initial guaranteed bulb when the last mech boss falls
- `NPC.OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime(int gameEventId)` — `Terraria/NPC.cs:79560`. Cases 16/17/18 (the three mech-boss game events, `NPC.cs:79578-79585`):
```csharp
case 16: case 17: case 18:
    LanternNight.NextNightIsLanternNight = true;
    if (Main.netMode != 1 && Main.hardMode && downedMechBoss1 && downedMechBoss2 && downedMechBoss3)
        WorldGen.GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated();
```
- `WorldGen.GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated()` — `WorldGen.cs:74180-74248`:
  - Search box: X range = jungle-side half of the map offset from `Main.dungeonX` (`num2/num3` at 74183-74189), Y from `Main.worldSurface` down to `Main.UnderworldLayer` (74190-74194).
  - 2500 attempts (`num5 = 2500`, 74195). Three tiers of desperation: `flag = num5 < 500` (allow spawning on Jungle Grass seed tile 59, and enter "forceBulb" mode), `flag2 = num5 < 200` (allow inactive tiles).
  - For each attempt it walks up from a random point until it finds tile 60 (Jungle Grass) — or 59 when `flag` (74206-74227).
  - Tries `AttemptToGeneratePlanteraBulbAt` at `(X, Y-1)`, `(X-1, Y-1)`, `(X+1, Y-1)` (74233-74244); returns on first success (so exactly **one** bulb is created by this call).
- `WorldGen.AttemptToGeneratePlanteraBulbAt(i, j, forceBulb)` — `WorldGen.cs:74250-74329`:
  - Forced mode (`forceBulb`, i.e. last 500 attempts): scores a 4×4 neighborhood (`k,l ∈ [-2,2)`, 74255-74292) — +1 per tile 59/60, −1 per other active tile; instant fail (−1) if any container/painting/door/tree/boulder etc. Requires score ≥ 12 (74293-74296). Then clears the 4×4, converts the outer ring to Jungle Grass 60 (74298-74309), places the bulb via `PlaceJunglePlant(i, j, 238, 0, 0, inheritPaint: true)` (74302), frames and `NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, i, j, 8)` (74304-74308).
  - Normal mode: just `PlaceJunglePlant(i, j, 238, 0, 0, inheritPaint: true)` + framing + `SendTileSquare(-1, i, j, 5)` (74316-74323).
- **No bulb is generated during world generation.** The only caller of `GeneratePlanteraBulbOnAllMechsDefeated` in the whole tree is `NPC.cs:79584`; there is no `generateWorld`-pass call.

### B. Ongoing bulb growth (the real spawner) — `WorldGen.UpdateWorld_GrassGrowth`
- Called from `WorldGen.UpdateWorld_UndergroundTile(...)` with `underground: true` — `WorldGen.cs:73334` (only for `TileID.Sets.SpreadUnderground` types, 73331-73334).
- The underground pass iterates `(Main.maxTilesX * Main.maxTilesY) * num3` random tiles per tick, where `num3 = 1.5E-05 * worldUpdateRate` — `WorldGen.cs:71544` and 71624-71633; `worldUpdateRate = Math.Min(Main.desiredWorldTilesUpdateRate, 24)` (`WorldGen.cs:72056-72064`); tile Y is picked in `[worldSurface-1, maxTilesY-20)` (`WorldGen.cs:71627`) — i.e. **below the surface only**.
- Bulb block, `case 60:` (Jungle Grass) — `WorldGen.cs:74480-74512`:
```csharp
if (flag || genRand.Next(25) != 0 || Main.tile[i, minJ].liquid != 0) break;      // 74482
if (Main.hardMode && NPC.downedMechBoss1 && downedMechBoss2 && downedMechBoss3
    && genRand.Next(60) == 0) {                                                    // 74486
    // scan 300x300 area (m,n in [i-150, i+150) step 2) for existing type 238     // 74489-74500
    // if none found (flag4):
    PlaceJunglePlant(i, minJ, 238, 0, 0, inheritPaint: true);                     // 74503
    SquareTileFrame(i, minJ); SquareTileFrame(i+2, minJ); SquareTileFrame(i-1, minJ);
    if (Main.tile[i, minJ].type == 238 && Main.netMode == 2)
        NetMessage.SendTileSquare(-1, i, minJ, 5);                                 // 74509
}
```
Exact conditions: hardmode **and all three mechs downed**, on an underground Jungle Grass tile (60) whose above-tile is air/liquid-free and did not just grow a plant (`flag`), gated by `genRand.Next(25)` **and** `genRand.Next(60)` (≈1/1500 per grass update per tile), with a **150-tile exclusion radius** (no other bulb within a 150×150 box, sampled every 2 tiles). There is **no global bulb counter** — the radius scan is the only cap, and there is **no check on `downedPlantBoss`** (see §10).

---

## 3. Breaking the bulb → Plantera spawns

Handled by **`WorldGen.CheckJunglePlant(int i, int j, int type)`** — `WorldGen.cs:47956` (invoked from `TileFrame`, `WorldGen.cs:86219-86223`, after any of the 2×2 group's tiles is broken or the grass anchor below is removed). It bails immediately if `destroyObject` is set (47959-47962, anti-recursion).

For type 238, the group is verified (2×2 complete, `SolidTile` Jungle Grass 60 below both columns — `WorldGen.cs:47994-48008`). If the group is damaged/unsupported (`flag == true`):
- `WorldGen.cs:48013-48031`: finds the **nearest player** by Manhattan pixel distance (`|dx|+|dy|`, 48019-48027) and:
```csharp
if (num8 / 16f < 50f)              // within 50 tiles (Manhattan, in tiles)
    NPC.SpawnOnPlayer(plr, 262);   // WorldGen.cs:48030
```
- Then it `KillTile`s the remaining 2×2 tiles with `destroyObject` guard (`WorldGen.cs:48038-48053`). No item drops, no special sound beyond normal tile break.

**So Plantera does NOT spawn at the bulb position** — it spawns via `NPC.SpawnOnPlayer` on the nearest player. `NPC.SpawnOnPlayer(int plr, int npcType, ...)` — `Terraria/NPC.cs:81273`:
- Hard block: `if (Main.netMode == 1 || (npcType == 262 && AnyNPCs(262))) return;` — `NPC.cs:81275-81279` (only one Plantera at a time, server-side only).
- General path (`NPC.cs:81364-81434`): up to 500 outer × 50 inner attempts to find an off-screen, non-safe, tile-supported spot in `Spawner.GetSpawnArea(player)`; on the final attempt it forces a spot. Then `SpawnBoss(x, y, 262, plr)`.
- `NPC.SpawnBoss` — `NPC.cs:81457`: `NewNPC(..., 262, Start:1, Target:plr)`, `Main.npc[..].timeLeft *= 20` (= 750×20 = **15000 ticks**, 81484), `NetMessage.SendData(23)` on server (81487-81490), and the default-case broadcast `NetworkText.FromKey("Announcement.HasAwoken", ...)` → **"Plantera has awoken!"** (`NPC.cs:81500-81506`).

---

## 4. Plantera NPC 265 (actually **262** in 1456)

### SetDefaults — `NPC.SetDefaults`, `Terraria/NPC.cs:12311-12325`
```csharp
else if (type == 262) {
    noTileCollide = true;
    width = 86; height = 86;
    aiStyle = 51;
    damage = 50; defense = 14; lifeMax = 30000;
    HitSound = SoundID.NPCHit1; DeathSound = SoundID.NPCDeath1;
    knockBackResist = 0f;
    value = Item.buyPrice(0, 15);      // 15 gold
    noGravity = true; boss = true; npcSlots = 16f;
}
```
- aiStyle **51**. Notably there is **no `music` field** in this build — music is selected globally (see §9).
- Difficulty/world tweaks that touch 262:
  - For-the-Worthy: `scale *= 1.3f` — `NPC.cs:17962-17964` (inside `ScaleStats_ByDifficulty_Tweaks`, method at 18118? — the block at 17962 is in the getGoodWorld stat-tweak chain).
  - Master-mode boss-life tweaks: `NPC.cs:18324-18328` (`num2 = 0.7`, `num3 *= CommonMasterBossLifeReduction`, `num = 1.15` for 262; and 18330-18333 for 264).
  - Player-count scaling applies the balance multiplier for 262 and 264 — `NPC.cs:18604-18610` (`ScaleStats_ByPlayerCount`).
- Animation: `NPC.cs:73239-73260` — frame advances every 7 ticks (`frameCounter > 6.0`); **phase 1** rows 0-3 (`life > lifeMax/2`), **phase 2** rows 4-8.
- Boss-head rotation uses `rotation` — `NPC.cs:7593-7597`; boss head texture slot 11 — `NPCID.cs:4861`.

### AI — `NPC.AI()`, `aiStyle == 51`, `Terraria/NPC.cs:31873-32235`

Setup / lifecycle:
- `TargetClosest()`; if target player is dead → `flag51 = flag50 = true` (31876-31881).
- Despawn prevention: while within **4800 px** of the target, `timeLeft` is reset to `activeTime` (**750**, `NPC.cs:6106`) — `NPC.cs:31885-31892`. Otherwise the normal `timeLeft` countdown applies (SpawnBoss had multiplied it by 20).
- Registers itself as the global boss: `NPC.plantBoss = whoAmI;` — `NPC.cs:31891` (field `NPC.cs:6054`; reset to −1 by `Main` when no 262 active, `Terraria/Main.cs:18023-18026`).
- On first AI tick spawns **3 Hooks (263)** at its own center — `NPC.cs:31893-31898`.

Movement core (31908-32025):
- Computes the mean position of all aiStyle-52 NPCs (the 3 hooks), `NPC.cs:31908-31922`.
- Base max speed `num793 = 2.5f`, accel `num794 = 0.025f` — `NPC.cs:31919-31920`.
  - `life < lifeMax/2` → `num793 = 5f`, `num794 = 0.05f` (31922-31926).
  - `life < lifeMax/4` → `num793 = 7f` (31927-31930).
- **Enrage (flag50)** — `NPC.cs:31930-31935`:
```csharp
if (!Main.player[target].ZoneJungle
    || Main.player[target].position.Y < Main.worldSurface * 16.0
    || Main.player[target].position.Y > Main.UnderworldLayer * 16)
{ flag50 = true; num793 += 8f; num794 = 0.15f; }
```
  i.e. target not in jungle **or** above the surface **or** below Underworld layer → enraged: +8 max speed, accel 0.15. (No color/visual change; the buff is purely speed/accel/defense/damage.)
- Expert: `num793 += 1f; num793 *= 1.1f; num794 += 0.01f; num794 *= 1.1f;` — 31936-31942.
- For-the-Worthy: `num793 *= 1.15f; num794 *= 1.15f;` — 31943-31946.
- If target dead (`flag51`): direction reversed and `num793 += 8f` — 31952-31956.
- Steering: target point = hooks' mean + clamped vector toward the player; clamp distance `num798 = 500` px, `+350` enraged, `+150` expert (31958-31970). Then velocity is lerped toward the desired velocity by `num794` per axis (31994-32021). `rotation = atan2(toPlayer) + 1.57f` (32023-32025).

### Phase 1 (`life > lifeMax/2`) — `NPC.cs:32026-32132`
- `defense = 36; int num801 = 50;` enraged → `defense *= 2` (72) and `num801 *= 2` (100); `damage = GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(num801)` — 32028-32036. (`GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty` = `normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(difficulty)`, `NPC.cs:7015-7017`; curve Journey 0.5 → Master 3.0 → Legendary 5.333, `Terraria.DataStructures/GameDifficultyData.cs:61`.)
- Shoot timer `localAI[1] += 1f` per tick, plus +1 for each of the HP thresholds 0.9/0.8/0.7/0.6 (32042-32053), **+3 enraged** (32054-32057), **+1 expert** (32058-32060), **+1 ftw** (32066-32068). In expert, `justHit && Main.rand.Next(2)==0` sets `localAI[3] = 1f` which forces the next shot to ignore line-of-sight (32061-32064, consumed at 32077-32081).
- Fire when `localAI[1] > 80f` → reset to 0 (32073-32076). Requires `Collision.CanHit` (or the `localAI[3]` override) — 32077-32081.
- Shot spec (32086-32130):
  - Speed `num802 = 15f` (expert 17f), normalized toward the player's center (32088-32097).
  - Default: type **275 Seed**, damage base **22** (`num806 = 22`, 32098).
  - If `life < 80%` and `Main.rand.Next(maxValue2) == 0` (`maxValue2 = 4`, expert **2**): type **276 Poison Seed**, damage base **27**, and `localAI[1] = -30f` (extra 30-tick cooldown) — 32104-32108.
  - Else if `life < 80%` and `Main.rand.Next(maxValue3) == 0` (`maxValue3 = 8`, expert **6**): type **277 Thorn Ball**, damage base **31**, `localAI[1] = -120f` — 32109-32113.
  - Enraged: `num806 *= 2` (32115-32117).
  - `num806 = GetAttackDamage_ForProjectiles(num806, num806 * 0.9f)` (Classic→Expert lerp, `NPC.cs:7031-7034`) — 32119.
  - Spawn at `center + velocity*3`, `Projectile.NewProjectile(..., num807, num806, 0f, Main.myPlayer)` — 32124-32126.
  - `timeLeft = 300` for everything except Thorn Ball (277 keeps its default) — 32127-32130.

### Phase transition at **50% HP** (`life <= lifeMax/2`) — `NPC.cs:32134-32235`
- `defense = 10; int num809 = 70;` enraged → `defense *= 4` (40) and `num809 *= 2` (140); `damage = GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(num809)` — 32134-32142.
- Tentacles spawn once (`localAI[0] == 1f` → 2f), `NPC.cs:32145-32170`:
  - `num810 = 8` tentacles (264) at Plantera's center; **+6 in ftw (14 total)** — 32147-32154.
  - **Expert only:** for each active Hook (aiStyle 52), spawn `num810/2 - 1 = 3` extra tentacles with `ai[3] = hookIndex + 1` (i.e. attached to that hook) — 32155-32167. (Total expert: 8 + 3×3 = 17.)
- Expert tentacle top-up: `Main.expertMode && Main.rand.Next(60) == 0` → if fewer than 8 free tentacles (`ai[3]==0`), spawn one more when `Main.rand.Next((count+1)*10) <= 1` — 32171-32185.
- Phase-2 gore burst once (`localAI[2] == 0f`): Gore 378, 379, 380 — 32187-32193.
- Spore launch: timer `localAI[1] += 1f` (+1 for each of 0.4/0.3/0.2/0.1 HP thresholds, 32200-32212); when `localAI[1] >= 350f` — 32215:
  - speed `num819 = 8f` aimed at the player with `±10` px randomization and gravity-compensation (`num821 = |dx*0.2|` subtracted only if aiming downward), 32216-32229;
  - `NewNPC(..., 265)` at center, sets velocity, `netUpdate = true`, resets timer — 32231-32235.

Despawn on player death: Plantera does not hard-despawn; with a dead target it flies away (31952-31956) and `timeLeft` no longer refreshes once out of the 4800 px ring, so it despawns by timer.

## 5. Hook (263) / Tentacle (264) / Spore (265) AI

### Hook 263 — SetDefaults `NPC.cs:12328-12340`
```csharp
noTileCollide = true; noGravity = true; width = 40; height = 40;
aiStyle = 52; damage = 60; defense = 24; lifeMax = 4000;
dontTakeDamage = true;                       // invulnerable
HitSound = SoundID.NPCHit1; DeathSound = SoundID.NPCDeath1;
```
AI `aiStyle == 52` — `NPC.cs:32236-32406`:
- If `plantBoss < 0` → `StrikeNPCNoInteraction(9999, 0f, 0)` + `netUpdate` (self-destruct when Plantera is gone) — 32242-32247.
- Enrage mirror: `!target.ZoneJungle || target.Y < worldSurface*16 || target.Y > UnderworldLayer*16 || targetDead` → `localAI[0] -= 4f; flag53 = true;` — 32248-32256.
- Retarget timer `localAI[0] -= 1f` per tick, −2 extra when boss < 50% HP, −2 more < 25%, −6 enraged (32263-32278). If any sibling hook is moving, `localAI[0] = Main.rand.Next(60, 300)` (32280-32288). When ≤ 0 → new anchor: `localAI[0] = Main.rand.Next(300, 600)`, then up to 1000 tries to pick a tile within `20 + 100*(attempt/1000)` tiles of the target (or midpoint when `ai[0]==0`, or below the boss if the player is dead) that is solid, or has a wall past attempt 500 / boss below 50% (32291-32346). Below 50% boss HP, 1/6 chance to anchor exactly in the player's tile if it has a wall (32321-32331). Anchor stored in `ai[0]`/`ai[1]`, `netUpdate`.
- Travel speed toward anchor: `6f`, 8f below 50%, 10f below 25%; +1 expert, +1 more expert below 50%; **×2 enraged, ×2 player-dead** — 32352-32374. Within `12 + speed` px it snaps (`velocity = delta`) — 32383-32388.
- For-the-Worthy only: when arriving at an anchor, `WorldGen.SpawnPlanteraThorns(base.Center)` (32377-32381; thorn planter at `WorldGen.cs:76329`: 3-5 thorns of 15-30 tiles of tile 655).
- `rotation = atan2(toBoss) - 1.57f` — 32402-32406.

### Tentacle 264 — SetDefaults `NPC.cs:12342-12353`
```csharp
width = 24; height = 24; aiStyle = 53; damage = 60; defense = 20; lifeMax = 1000;
HitSound = NPCHit1; DeathSound = NPCDeath1; noGravity = true; noTileCollide = true;
```
AI `aiStyle == 53` — `NPC.cs:32407-32524`:
- ftw: 1/10 chance per tick to set `reflectsProjectiles = true` — 32409-32420.
- Self-destruct if `plantBoss < 0` — 32422-32427; also `active = false` if its attach target is gone — 32452-32456.
- Attach target: Plantera by default; `ai[3] > 0` → hook index `ai[3]-1` — 32429-32433.
- Wobble: every `Main.rand.Next(120, 480)` ticks pick new offset `ai[0], ai[1] ∈ [-100, 100]` — 32435-32444.
- Spring parameters: accel `num841 = 0.2f`, leash `num842 = 200f`; +100 below 25% boss HP, +100 below 10% (32447-32453); expert: leash += `300 * (1 - life/lifeMax)` and accel += 0.3 (32454-32459); ftw accel += 4 (32460-32463).
- Movement: spring toward `target center + (ai[0], ai[1])` scaled to leash length, velocity clamped to ±8 — 32465-32511; rotation from movement direction (32513-32524).

### Spore 265 — SetDefaults `NPC.cs:12355-12369`
```csharp
width = 18; height = 18; aiStyle = 50; damage = 70; defense = 0; lifeMax = 1;
HitSound = NPCHit1; DeathSound = NPCDeath1; noGravity = true; noTileCollide = true;
knockBackResist = 0f; npcSlots = 0f;
```
AI `aiStyle == 50` — `NPC.cs:31807-31871`:
- `EncourageDespawn(5)` — dies ~5 ticks after leaving relevance (31808).
- `velocity.Y += 0.02f`, terminal fall `1f` (31825-31834); slight horizontal homing `±0.1f` per tick (double in expert), capped at ±5 (31835-31861); `rotation = velocity.X * 0.2f` (31863).
- In `NPCID.Sets.NeedsExpertScaling` and `ProjectileNPC` — `NPCID.cs:4799-4801`, so its 70 damage scales with difficulty.

## 6. Projectiles

SetDefaults — `Terraria/Projectile.cs:3346-3369`:
- **275 SeedPlantera**: `alpha = 255`, 14×14, `aiStyle = 1` (gravity/arcing), hostile.
- **276 PoisonSeedPlantera**: `alpha = 255`, 14×14, `aiStyle = 1`, hostile.
- **277 ThornBall**: `alpha = 255`, 38×38, `aiStyle = 14` (bouncing ball), hostile; collision box 26×26 (`Projectile.cs:18544-18548`).

Behavior:
- 275/276 fade in (`alpha -= 30`), gain gravity `velocity.Y += 0.025f` after 35 ticks of flight — `Projectile.cs:54318-54329`. **Expert homing**: velocity lerps toward the closest player at speed 18 with weight 70, floor 14 — `Projectile.cs:54330-54345`. 2-frame animation — `Projectile.cs:52129-52140`. Launch sound `SoundID.Item17` — `Projectile.cs:52458-52467`.
- **276 poison on hit**: expert → always `ApplyBuffTo(player, 20, Main.rand.Next(120, 540))`; normal → 50% chance, `Main.rand.Next(180, 420)` — `Projectile.cs:11527-11535` (buff 20 = Poison).
- **277 Thorn Ball bouncing**: on tile collision `velocity.X = lastVelocity.X * -0.9f`; vertical bounce only if `lastVelocity.Y > 3f` → `* -0.9f` — `Projectile.cs:18306-18314`. Fades in — `Projectile.cs:23297-23306`. **Expert homing**: lerp toward the closest player at speed 12, weight 200, speed cap 16 — `Projectile.cs:23307-23316`.
- Kill effects for 275/276: sound 0 + 5 dusts (type 7) — `Projectile.cs:71329-71337`.
- Pierce/penetrate: defaults (penetrate 1) — destroyed on first player hit.

## 7. Death, drops, flags

### Flag set
- `NPC.DoDeathEvents` `case 262:` — `Terraria/NPC.cs:80231-80242`:
```csharp
bool num3 = downedPlantBoss;
SetEventFlagCleared(ref downedPlantBoss, 12);      // gameEventId 12
if (!num3)
    ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(NetworkText.FromKey(Lang.misc[33].Key), ChatColors.World);
```
  `SetEventFlagCleared` (`NPC.cs:79568-79576`) invokes `OnGameEventClearedForTheFirstTime` (Lantern Night etc.).
- Field: `public static bool downedPlantBoss = false;` — `NPC.cs:6280`. **There is no `Main.planteraDefeated` in vanilla** (grep over the tree returns nothing).
- Sync: on any boss death `NetMessage.SendData(7)` (world flags packet) — `NPC.cs:80313-80317`; the flag bit itself is `bitsByte6[7] = NPC.downedPlantBoss` — `Terraria/NetMessage.cs:278`. Persistence: `Terraria.IO/WorldFile.cs:1290` (write), `:2084` (read), `:3526` (legacy load).
- Also: boss potion/heart drop — `DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts` (`NPC.cs:79746`) maps 262 → potion 499 (Greater Healing Potion), stack 5-15, plus 5-9 Heart items (58) — `NPC.cs:79778-79792`; invoked from the `if (boss)` block at `NPC.cs:80309-80317` together with `DoDeathEvents_CelebrateBossDeath` (defeat announcement).

### Loot (data-driven: `Main.ItemDropSolver.TryDropping(info)` — `NPC.cs:79726-79742`, rules in `Terraria.GameContent.ItemDropRules/ItemDropDatabase.cs`)
`RegisterBoss_Plantera()` — `ItemDropDatabase.cs:409-432` (registered from `RegisterBosses` at :295):
- **Always:** `BossBag(3328)` (expert) — :412.
- Master: `MasterModeCommonDrop(4934)` = PlanteraMasterTrophy (relic) — :413; `MasterModeDropOnAllPlayers(4806, rng)` = PlanteraPetItem (Spore Sac pet? no — 4806 = PlanteraPetItem, "Nematode"? — it's the Plantera pet light pet) — :414.
- **Non-expert** (`LeadingConditionRule(new Conditions.NotExpert())`, :415-417):
  - PlanteraMask 2109 — 1/7 (:419)
  - **TempleKey 1141 — always** (:420)
  - Seedling 1182 — 1/20 (:421)
  - TheAxe 1305 — 1/50 (:422)
  - PygmyStaff 1157 — 1/4 (:423)
  - ThornHook 3021 — 1/10 (:424)
  - Sub-rule `Conditions.FirstTimeKillingPlantera` (`Conditions.cs:354-359` → `!NPC.downedPlantBoss`), :418/426:
    - on success → GrenadeLauncher 758 with onSuccess RocketI 771 ×50-150 (:425, 427-428);
    - on failure → `OneFromRulesRule(1, …)` exactly one of: GrenadeLauncher(+rockets), VenusMagnum 1255, NettleBurst 788, LeafBlower 1178, FlowerPow 1259, WaspGun 1155, Seedler 3018, FlowerWhip 5477 (:429).
- Trophy: `RegisterBossTrophies` — `ItemDropDatabase.cs:886`: `RegisterToNPC(262, ItemDropRule.ByCondition(condition, 1370, 10))` → **PlanteraTrophy 1/10**.
- Skyblock-only: `NPCLoot_DropLihzahrdStuff()` when `WorldGen.Skyblock.noTemple` — `NPC.cs:79688-79690`.

### Treasure bag 3328 contents — `Player.OpenBossBag` `case 3328:` — `Terraria/Player.cs:7077-7135`
- `TryGettingDevArmor`, then: mask 2109 1/7; **TempleKey 1141 always**; **SporeSac 3336 always** (:7087); Seedling 1182 1/15; TheAxe 1305 1/20; PygmyStaff 1157 1/2; ThornHook 3021 1/10; then `switch (Main.rand.Next(8))`: 0 → GrenadeLauncher + RocketI 50-150; 1 VenusMagnum; 2 NettleBurst; 3 LeafBlower; 4 FlowerPow; 5 WaspGun; 6 Seedler; 7 FlowerWhip (:7112-7134).

## 8. Downstream consumers of `NPC.downedPlantBoss`

**Gate expression style**: `NPC.downedPlantBoss` (there is no `planteraDefeated`).

Temple access / Golem:
- **Lihzahrd Altar (tile 237) + Power Cell** — `Player.cs:32303-32324`: cell (item 1293) is consumed and Golem (245) spawned only `if (!NPC.AnyNPCs(245) && Main.hardMode && NPC.downedPlantBoss)`. Pre-Plantera the altar does nothing (cell not consumed).
- **Actuator bypass blocked** — `Terraria/Wiring.cs:3216-3220` (`Wiring.DeActive`): `if (tile.type == 226 && j > Main.worldSurface && !NPC.downedPlantBoss) return;` (Lihzahrd Brick can't be actuated open pre-Plantera).
- **Teleporter into temple blocked** — `Wiring.cs:1554-1557`: teleporter on tile 235 refuses when `tile.wall == 87 && j > Main.worldSurface && !NPC.downedPlantBoss`.
- **Rod of Discord / teleport items** — `Player.cs:44891-44894`: destination rejected if `Main.tile[x,y].wall == 87 && !NPC.downedPlantBoss && (Main.remixWorld || y > Main.worldSurface)`.
- Generic teleport spot check — `Terraria/Utils.cs:520`: `tile.wall == 87 && num5 > Main.worldSurface && !NPC.downedPlantBoss` → invalid.

Dungeon "hardmode" state & post-Plantera spawns:
- `NPC.hardDungeon = downedPlantBoss && Main.hardMode;` — `NPC.cs:291` (per-spawn-context snapshot). Consumed in `NPC.SpawnNPC` dungeon branch — `NPC.cs:2568-2633`: Bone Lee 287 (1/30, :2568), Skeleton Commando 293 / Skeleton Sniper 291 / Tactical Skeleton 292 (1/15 per brick variant, :2573-2583), Paladin 290 (1/35, only if none and brick variant 0, :2588-2592), Giant Cursed Skull 289 (1/30, :2593-2597), Ragged Caster family 281-286 (1/20, :2598-2611), and the 269-280 pool gets 2/3 of the spawns (:2616-2630).
- Dungeon Spirit: `DoDeathEvents_SummonDungeonSpirit` — `NPC.cs:79863-79878`: `lifeMax > 100 && type != 288 && value > 0 && HasPlayerTarget && Main.hardMode && downedPlantBoss && Main.player[target].ZoneDungeon`, 1/13 (expert 1/9) luck roll while in dungeon wall.

Solar Eclipse unlock — `NPC.cs:3466-3502` (eclipse branch): Mothron 477 (1/80), Psycho 466 (1/5), Nailhead 463 (1/20), Deadly Sphere 467 (1/20, ≤2), Dr. Man Fly 468 (1/7), Butcher 460 (1/5) — all `downedPlantBoss &&`.

Prismatic Lacewing (Empress summon) — `NPC.cs:3948-3951`: `downedPlantBoss && (remixWorld || (!Main.dayTime && Main.time < 16200.0)) && surfaceSpawn && RollLuck(10) == 0 && !AnyNPCs(661)` → spawn NPC 661 (EmpressButterfly).

Town NPC / shops:
- Cyborg (209) can spawn: `Main.hardMode && NPC.downedPlantBoss` — `Main.cs:65355-65357` and `Main.cs:65493` (two spawn-context copies).
- Demolitionist sells Land Mine 937: `Main.hardMode && NPC.downedPlantBoss && NPC.downedPirates` — `Terraria/Chest.cs:1731-1734`.
- Truffle sells Autohammer 1551: `NPC.downedPlantBoss` — `Chest.cs:2095-2098`.
- Witch Doctor post-Plantera stock: `Chest.cs:2599-2620` (`Main.hardMode && NPC.downedPlantBoss` → PygmyNecklace 1158 (night), TikiMask/Shirt/Pants 1159-1161, HerculesBeetle 1167 (jungle), VialofVenom 1339, LeafWings 1162 (night+jungle at :2613-2616), TikiTotem 1171).
- Painter graveyard stock Echo Coating 5344: `NPC.downedPlantBoss || NPC.AnyNPCs(124)` — `Chest.cs:2448-2451`.
- Zoologist sells MudBud 4701: `NPC.downedPlantBoss` — `Chest.cs:3326-3328`.
- Traveling Merchant: post-Plantera adds WispDye 2878, PixieDye 2879, UnicornWispDye 2884, InfernalWispDye 2885 — `Player.cs:55768-55773`.
- Loot-sim / emote / hairstyle / pylon files also read the flag (`SimulationConditionSetters.cs`, `EmoteBubble.cs`, `HairstyleUnlocksHelper.cs`, `TeleportHelpers.cs`, `TeleportPylonsSystem.cs`, `UIEmotesMenu.cs`) — cosmetic/UI only.

Difficulty scaling:
- `NPC.cs:53503-53507` (expert/master scaling): `num3 *= 0.985f; num2 += 0.15f; defense += 8;`.
- `NPC.cs:92896-92898` (damage scaling ladder): `num12 += 0.15f;`.
- `NPC.cs:18481-18483` (`ScaleStats_ForExpertHardmode`): weak-NPC normalization threshold `num2 = 80` → `+20` (=100) after Plantera.
- Pot loot scaling: `WorldGen.SpawnThingsFromPot` `WorldGen.cs:57739-57741`: coin amount `num16 *= 1.1f` per boss downed, incl. Plantera.
- Biome spread halved post-Plantera: `WorldGen.hardUpdateWorld` `WorldGen.cs:69763-69766`: `if ((NPC.downedPlantBoss && genRand.Next(2) != 0) || !AllowedToSpreadInfections) return;`.
- Item tooltip ("cursed by Plantera"-style sets) — `Main.cs:20787`.

## 9. Music / SFX

- **No per-NPC `music` field in this build.** Selection is `Main.UpdateAudio_DecideOnNewMusic()` — `Main.cs:12101`:
  - `case 262: case 263: case 264: num3 = 6;` — `Main.cs:12232-12236` (hooks and tentacles count too; Spore 265 does not).
  - Detection box: 5000 px square centered on the NPC vs the screen rectangle (`num2 = 5000`, `Main.cs:12153/12257-12261`).
  - `else if (flag7) { newMusic = 24; }` — `Main.cs:12494-12496`. `24 = MusicID.Plantera` (`MusicID.cs:60`). (`MusicID.Boss5 = 25` is not used for Plantera.)
- Hit/death sounds: `HitSound = SoundID.NPCHit1`, `DeathSound = SoundID.NPCDeath1` — `NPC.cs:12320-12321` (same for 263/264/265 at :12338-12339, :12350-12351, :12362-12363).
- Spawn announcement: `Announcement.HasAwoken` broadcast — `NPC.cs:81500-81506`. Defeat announcement: `DoDeathEvents_CelebrateBossDeath` — `NPC.cs:79824-79842`; first-kill flavor: `Lang.misc[33]` — `NPC.cs:80240`.

## 10. Bulb regrowth after Plantera

**Yes — spawning continues unchanged after Plantera is defeated.** The only gate in `WorldGen.UpdateWorld_GrassGrowth` (`WorldGen.cs:74486`) is `Main.hardMode && NPC.downedMechBoss1 && NPC.downedMechBoss2 && NPC.downedMechBoss3 && genRand.Next(60) == 0`; `NPC.downedPlantBoss` is never consulted. Same rate (1/25 × 1/60 per underground jungle-grass update), same 150-tile exclusion radius, same anchor rule (2×2 on Jungle Grass 60, air above, `PlaceJunglePlant(..., 238, 0, 0, inheritPaint: true)`). The only Plantera-flag-adjacent world change is the biome-spread reduction in `hardUpdateWorld` (`WorldGen.cs:69763`), which is unrelated to bulb growth.
